<script>
    window.addEventListener('load', function () {
        var surveyResponse = '';

        if (surveyResponse === null || surveyResponse === "" || surveyResponse === undefined) {
            return;
        }

        const obj = JSON.parse(surveyResponse.replace(/&quot;/ig, '"'));

        var data = {
             "response": {
                "response": obj.response,
                "comment": null,
                "ticketId": obj.ticketNumber
             }
        };

        var url = window.location.origin + '/SatisfactionSurvey/SaveResponse?id=' + obj.questionId +"&chatWidgetId=undefined";

        $.ajax({
            url: url,
            type: 'PATCH',
            data: JSON.stringify(data),
            contentType: "application/json",
            success: function (response) { },
            error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                console.error('PATCH request failed:', status, error);
            }
        });
    });

</script>

                let cookieName = 'registerTrialLoginMixPanel';

                    mixpanel.people.set({

        <script>
        if (hasWhatsAppCache()) {
            var whatsappCache = localStorage.getItem("hasWhatsapp");
            pushToDataLayer(JSON.parse(whatsappCache).hasWhatsapp);
        } else {
            var loggedUserToken = Cookies.get("Bearer");
            var settings = {
                "async": true,
                "crossDomain": true,
                "url": "https://socialchannel.app.movidesk.com/api/v1/whatsapp/consumption/get-numbers",
                "method": "GET",
                "headers": {
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    "Authorization": `Bearer ${loggedUserToken}`
                },
            }

            $.ajax(settings)
                .done(function (response) {
                    const hasWhatsapp = response.length > 0 ? true : false;
                    setWhatsAppCache(hasWhatsapp);
                    pushToDataLayer(hasWhatsapp);
                })
                .fail(function (response) {
                    const hasWhatsapp = false
                    setWhatsAppCache(hasWhatsapp);
                    pushToDataLayer(hasWhatsapp);
                });
        }

        function hasWhatsAppCache() {
            var whatsappCache = localStorage.getItem("hasWhatsapp");

            if (!whatsappCache || whatsappCache.expiry < new Date().getTime())
                return false;

            return true;
        }

        function setWhatsAppCache(hasWhatsApp) {
            localStorage.setItem("hasWhatsapp", JSON.stringify({
                hasWhatsapp: hasWhatsApp,
                expiry: new Date().getTime() + 80000000
            }));
        }

        function pushToDataLayer(hasWhatsapp) {
            window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
            window.dataLayer.push({
                'Whatsapp' : hasWhatsapp
            });
        }
        </script>
